# Native dependency inventory (H1a)

This inventory covers the supplied 87 `node` and 33 `node_internal` quarry modules at fn1 `6521f18c4`. It inventories dependencies and replacement evidence; it does not run the natives or implement the H1b adapter.

`inventory.py` uses only Python's standard library. Its AST pass reads the original sources without importing them; its `JUDGMENTS` dictionary records the reviewed representation sites and module decisions. Frozen copies of the supplied quarry lists and release-plan table make regeneration independent of the coordinator's scratch directory. Section 1 coverage is the mechanical inverse of that table's test column, including wildcard module names.

Regenerate the JSON with `python3 fn2/tests/harness/inventory.py --json > fn2/tests/harness/natives-inventory.json`. `--markdown` emits everything from the generated marker onward; keep this head when replacing that part. `--check` checks both artifacts, subject totals, dependency sites, row references and the mixed-module method partitions.

Dependency sites are qualified by `source`; `via` records imported fixture chains. Literal arguments and forwarding wrappers are resolved where possible. `dynamic` retains an unresolved expression and its site rather than inventing a value. Counts are module unions, including reachable shared helpers. `test_methods` expands static unittest inheritance; `declared_test_methods` counts definitions, including mixins.

The seven adapter operations are `start`, `stop`, `config`, `socket`, `control`, `kill_at`, and `observe`. `carry-with-adapter` includes modules with a public core whose cited representation assertions need replacement. `carry-socket-half` records the public methods separately from probes, mixed methods and source-only checks; it can expose a hidden probe even in the supplied `node` category. `rewrite-row` retains the named public obligation while replacing the old test design. A public-contract verdict identifies a carried observable, not approval of fn1's encoding or implementation as fn2's design.

Node starts also depend on the installed launcher, core layout, heap probe, deployed stack, pinned OpenSSL prefix and liveness obligations. These inherited requirements are counted. `require_node_greeting` must observe an existing NNTP/control/HTTP response or a named terminal outcome; a readiness announcement alone is insufficient. H1b must replace the outcome-table, protocol-table and environment-selector source dependencies, and preserve uncertainty separately from refusal.

Host-requirement counts describe what each module depends on, including tests needing rewrites. They do not imply that one item alone makes those modules pass. Old heap keys, storage layouts, fault-cut names and in-image probes require the public replacements listed below. No image availability, runtime skips or native assertions were exercised for H1a.

### What layer D and the host must serve

Ordered by modules depending on each item. Entries describe fn1 inputs: representation-specific selectors and removed verbs require the replacements in the judgment table, not blind implementation. The launcher also needs `packaging/fn`, the core-image layout, the heap probe and the shipped OpenSSL prefix (overridable by `FN_OPENSSL_PREFIX`). The adapter must redirect the outcome table and protocol-reply table dependencies.

### Module decisions and remaining judgment uncertainty

| Module | Decision | Reason | Uncertainty |
|---|---|---|---|
| accept_maintenance | carry-with-adapter | The test observes POST blockage and SIGTERM drain announcement under a fault stall, without inspecting the maintenance worker; adapt the stall hook and lifecycle observations. | none |
| admin | carry-socket-half | Preserve public administrative CLI/control outcomes. Rewrite .cfg/.stage names, numeric config-generation filenames, SBCL .core preflight and section injections; retire source lint. | Medium: exact generation counters are CLI diagnostics but section1 promises config semantics rather than fn1 record generations. IMAGE_READY preflight must change for all NativeAdminTests, including the otherwise public race test. |
| agent_wait | carry-socket-half | Separate host-source shape test plus public TLS NNTP/consumer/operator scenario; retain the scenario and replace source coupling. | CAPACITY=12 at 48 needs parameterization to fn2 operator waiter capacity. Cursor/report files at 103 and 234 are public consumer outputs, not store representation. |
| article_quiet | carry-with-adapter | POST/ARTICLE/BODY bytes and process output are externally observable. Adapt start/socket/observe; the historical ACL2 warning regression should remain an output hygiene check without requiring ACL2 internals. | none |
| article_slots | rewrite-row | All I/O is public, but the admission counts 30/7/8 are derived from fn1 packed article and heap-pool layout, including the 3947519-byte eight-slot profile. Re-express the same reserve-to-finish, saturation/refusal and eventual completion scenarios using fn2 explicit profile capacities; retain the concurrent NNTP transcript checks. | This is a public-wire test classified rewrite-row because its core numeric oracle is representation-derived, not because it runs ACL2. Fixture inheritance must not incorrectly mark unused ProfileFixture.frame as a dependency. |
| article_subject | rewrite-row | Two independent nodes receive relay variants and read bodies publicly, but the decisive identity assertions parse service-log article-subject/bytes-subject encodings and require fn1 domain tags. Retain relay invariance and distinct bytes using fn2 documented identity observables, with explicit replacement evidence for subject binding. | Subject encodings appear in the public service log but are not explicitly preserved by section 1. Identity binding must keep replacement coverage; simply dropping these assertions would weaken the test. |
| auth | carry-with-adapter | Keep AUTHINFO/access/refusal and live principal rebinding behavior. config supplies auth/control/log tables, control adapts direct operator and hybrid-sign argv, observe supplies run-once exits and service logs. | none |
| bounds_join | carry-with-adapter | Large article identity, fragmentation and restart checks are public; start/control/observe adapt profile and reference image. Mutex allocation measurement must be replaced by fn2 bounded-work/resource evidence. | Span reference test compares public bytes but requires an old reference image. It can remain differential via start(image=...), or become a fixed transcript fixture. |
| capacity_vector | rewrite-row | Preserve full-store obligation/release/reclaim/restart behavior, but fn1 reserve accounting, export layout exceptions, log cuts and inherited ACL2 BP request construction need replacement. | Quarry labels this node, but inherited setUp constructs BP request through Acl2Session. This is actual reachable dependency, not a reclassification of the supplied list. |
| checkpoint | rewrite-row | Socket/CLI preservation checks survive, but production compaction asserts numbered segments and old checkpoint phase output, and developer tests exercise retired generation verbs. | checkpoint clone and retired top-level checkpoint verbs are candidates for disposition, not settled removals; retain authored-content recovery coverage whichever verb replaces clone. |
| cold_line_deadline | carry-with-adapter | All assertions are NNTP HDR/XHDR/XPAT/NEWNEWS completion, deadlines and concurrent-reader service. Cache and owner implementation names occur only in comments. Needs start, socket, control initialization and stop. | none |
| cold_line_quanta | carry-with-adapter | Public NNTP complete replies and concurrent reader latency; the cache-size rationale is a comment, not an internal probe. Needs start, socket, control initialization and stop. | none |
| cold_off_loop | carry-with-adapter | The test asserts ARTICLE and other readers make progress while a developer selector holds a read. start(env) must translate FN_NATIVE_TEST_READ_STALL_FILE and observe supplies diagnostics; the marker lives under root, not in the store layout. | none |
| commit_log | rewrite-row | Preserve concurrent POST/restart bodies, public store maintenance success and cut outcomes. Several tests otherwise assert journal naming, physical segment sizes, transactions-directory absence, pipeline trace and fn1 cut names, so they need assertion rewrites. | none |
| compress | carry-with-adapter | All five methods assert public compressed NNTP exchanges or documented close/refusal diagnostics, with no Lisp/source probes. Requires start(run --once), socket, control account setup and observe exit/service log hooks. | Quarry node_internal is retained; comments cite decoder internals but the 689-command fixture is literal wire bytes and all tests are public-only. Confirm fn2 service-log refusal spellings as part of the carried COMPRESS contract. |
| conformance | carry-with-adapter | The NNTP, authentication, transit and CLI cases exercise public behavior. Adapt start/config/control/observe and the uncertainty selector through kill_at. The configured-record filename expectations need replacement with successful configuration plus readback; ordinary status counts and service logging remain public observations. | The external configured output includes a record=.cfg filename; it is observable but section 1 carries configuration behavior, not this storage naming. Four assertions need editing in addition to Node adaptation. |
| consumer_e2 | carry-socket-half | Four methods observe consumer CLI/control replies and cursors. The signed composite method additionally decodes concrete fn1 stxa/record values through ACL2; replace that half with the public consumer verifier. | none |
| consumer_exchange | carry-with-adapter | The consumer client owns its SQLite schema; node observations are public control/CLI. Adapter start and kill_at must provide after-submit stop, CONTROL-SUBMITTED announcement, and process death before reply. | The client SQLite assertions intentionally retain the shipped client schema; if E6 rewrites that client too, use its inspect/export APIs instead of SQL. This is not a node-store-layout dependency. |
| consumer_exchange_two_nodes | carry-socket-half | The revoked-author case drives public consumer/NNTP/CLI behavior. Exchange identity evidence also reads the client SQLite schema, and the BP exchange constructs its request through Acl2Session. | The direct SQL accesses concern fn-consumer, not the node store; still private representation and replaceable by public artifact/summary output. Only the BP test invokes an in-image ACL2 helper. |
| consumer_identity | carry-socket-half | NativeConsumerIdentityTests is a public implicit-TLS/consumer/CLI scenario. ConsumerIdentitySourceTests is a separate host-text assertion and retires; its internal_only_tests bucket is source-only and launches no image. | none |
| control | carry-socket-half | NativeControlTests mostly exercise public CLI, sockets, lifecycle and refusals. Gate-source tests retire; the production-selector byte snapshot and legacy-client ACL2 encoder need replacement. Source-only tests use internal_only_tests and do not launch an image. | Module import itself reads host/books to generate tables even for socket-only methods. H1b must replace these tables/isolated source tests; list labels classify method bodies and invoked helpers, not import-time contamination. |
| control_across_peers | carry-with-adapter | Public hybrid-author, control grants and NNTP observations across receiver death; kill_at must replace the direct process kill/wait/finish helper. | none |
| control_filing | carry-with-adapter | Assertions are public NNTP, hybrid CLI, and service-log contracts. Route explicit process death through kill_at and command argv through control; no store files or raw image probes. | none |
| credits | carry-with-adapter | Concurrent POST/reader outcomes remain public; start must expose a storage barrier stall, and fn1 fixed article-slot/reserve assumptions need fn2 profile mapping. | Memory 440 and exact count of simultaneous offered bodies need explicit fn2 resource/refusal mapping; socket behavior should not be lost with the old credit implementation. |
| dead_run | carry-socket-half | The timing method uses public IHAVE/cancel/GROUP and CLI configuration. The complexity method instruments saved-image catalog symbols via a Lisp count hook; its public workload survives but its counting proof must target fn2 work accounting. | The first method needs start_store_owner mapped to start and native_peer_add mapped to control/config. It does not inspect store layout merely by passing store_path as CLI argument. |

The essential order witness is fn1 DECODED-WINDOW token, fd/file and PARTIAL-FIXED-STORAGE trace syntax; merely adapting launch would not preserve its claim. | Section 1 names decoded_custody only in the classical-gate row; ARTICLE/HEAD/BODY plus OWN scenario evidence is the substantive replacement. The compression report is a listed top-level verb, but its usefulness as a fn2 decoded-read precondition needs review. |

This is not a wholesale representation-only module: its mixin and post-reclaim socket checks are public. | carry-with-adapter is coarse: most assertions are public, but config/fault adapters alone cannot satisfy lines 324-338 and 395-408. Preserve durable key lifecycle and refusal behavior, review replacement headroom assertions with layer D. |

| log_compaction | rewrite-row | Compaction, reclaim and recovery have useful public assertions, but every test additionally requires fn1 journal segment names, rotation cuts or checkpoint layout. | none |
| log_sink | rewrite-row | The blocked-stderr workload is valuable, but its sole native case mixes public POST/health/termination with queue counters and physical decision-journal entry accounting. Two source-only tests pin writer implementation and retire. | none |

| operator_walk | carry-with-adapter | Installed CLI, mission, TLS, peering, backup/move and crash recovery are public; adapt launcher/start/config/socket/control/observe/kill_at, and replace core-file package assertion. | The core hash equality at 152 must become shipped fn2 executable integrity. BP journal wire-file naming may be documented externally, but the release table commits to bundle bytes and machine behavior, not this layout. |

PublicNodeTests is opt-in against an external configured deployment. |
| over_cursor_cost | rewrite-row | The two wire OVER replies are reusable, but this measurement specifically requires fn1 owner mutex hold/cons allocation labels and binary store fixtures. | over_pins | carry-socket-half | Two independent slow/stopped ARTICLE reader methods observe public socket liveness and send-stalled behavior. The cold quantum test permits no held read and thus does not always witness concurrency under blocked storage. |
| over_window | carry-with-adapter | The assertion is a complete byte-identical OVER stream at two scheduling quanta. | own_cancel | carry-socket-half | The RFC 8315 cancel/relay/restart scenarios are public and separable.
